 All updates [/codex/changelog] General [/codex/changelog?type=general] ChatGPT desktop app [/codex/changelog?type=codex-app] Codex Mobile [/codex/changelog?type=codex-mobile] Codex CLI [/codex/changelog?type=codex-cli] 

 October 2026 [#month-2026-10] September 2026 [#month-2026-09] August 2026 [#month-2026-08] July 2026 [#month-2026-07] June 2026 [#month-2026-06] May 2026 [#month-2026-05] April 2026 [#month-2026-04] March 2026 [#month-2026-03] February 2026 [#month-2026-02] January 2026 [#month-2026-01] December 2025 [#month-2025-12] November 2025 [#month-2025-11] October 2025 [#month-2025-10] September 2025 [#month-2025-09] August 2025 [#month-2025-08] June 2025 [#month-2025-06] May 2025 [#month-2025-05] 

 October 2026 

 2026-10-29 

 Codex CLI 0.166.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.166.0

 View details 

New Features

Added keyboard-driven transcript selection with word, line, and page motions, integrated search, and configurable shortcuts. (#57221 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57221], #57222 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57222], #57223 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57223], #57224 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57224])

Rendered Markdown consistently in async questions, choices, answers, and transcript replies. (#57259 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57259], #57292 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57292])

Added GPT-6.1 Terra to the bundled, Vertex AI, and Amazon Bedrock model catalogs. (#57293 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57293])

Added codex queue --resume to resume unloaded threads after queueing a message. (#57232 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57232])

Added clearer safety-block explanations and selectable safer follow-up prompts in the TUI. (#57363 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57363], #57365 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57365])

Added codex doctor --network-only for focused configuration, connectivity, and WebSocket diagnostics. (#57606 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57606])

Bug Fixes

Preserved acknowledged approval and permission settings when resuming or forking threads. (#57227 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57227])

Preserved local image attachments when restoring killed composer text. (#57502 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57502])

Fixed standalone release cleanup on macOS when installations are in use or paths contain non-ASCII characters. (#57503 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57503])

Restored spawned descendant threads when unarchiving their parent. (#57635 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57635])

Fixed app-server thread startup and shutdown races and released completed runtimes promptly. (#57787 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57787], #57788 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57788])

Improved TUI reconnection recovery, including offline /quit and /exit handling. (#57820 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57820])

Preserved reasoning-token accounting across resume and fork to avoid premature compaction. (#57839 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57839])

Chores

Switched Bazel to source-built V8 with target-matched Rust bindings and native Windows MSVC archive support. (#57164 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57164], #57269 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57269], #57644 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57644], #57832 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57832])

Shipped the standalone executor in canonical CLI and app-server release packages. (#57837 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57837])

Standardized development and release builds on Node.js 24.22.1 and Rust 1.95.0. (#57168 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57168], #57373 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57373])

 2026-10-27 

 Codex CLI 0.165.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.165.0

 View details 

New Features

Added a built-in Google Cloud Vertex AI provider, configured with model_provider = "gcp". (#56265 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56265])

Added GPT-6.1 Terra to the model catalogs. (#57337 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/57337])

Added a repository filter to the session picker and directory grouping to the agent command center, making it easier to navigate sessions across linked worktrees. (#56308 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56308])

Added experimental attached thread forks to the app-server API. (#56351 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56351])

Enabled reasoning effort changes during a conversation by default for supported models. (#56163 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56163])

Enabled memory v2 by default for fresh profiles and added support for importing external-agent memories into it. (#56211 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56211], #56414 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56414])

Bug Fixes

Fixed fresh TUI sessions to honor the server’s default approval reviewer when no explicit override is provided. (#56090 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56090])

Kept TUI preferences editable and immediately applicable when execution settings are invalid. (#56091 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56091])

Cancelled remote MCP tool requests when their local response futures are dropped. (#56132 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56132])

Prevented queued agent messages from restarting work after an interrupt or a switch to Plan mode. (#56330 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56330])

Stopped retrying when a provider reports that a model was not found, preserving its error message. (#56445 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56445])

Closed a marketplace URL validation gap that could allow Git to connect to a different host than the one checked against allowed patterns. (#56263 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56263])

 2026-10-26 

 GPT-6.1 Terra in Work and Codex 

GPT-6.1 Terra is now available in ChatGPT Work and Codex on Plus,
Pro, Business, Enterprise, and Edu plans. It’s a fast, efficient model for
professional work that combines strong image understanding with precise
computer use.

Reason about images and screens, then act on what you find across your browser,
desktop, and apps. Terra also provides clear, direct writing that puts results
first and explains supporting details in plain language.

Choose GPT-6.1 Terra from the model list in Advanced in the ChatGPT desktop
app or ChatGPT Work on the web. Work also supports Terra on mobile. The model isn’t available in Chat and does not support UAE inference residency. Enterprise access depends on workspace
availability and administrator controls.

See Models [/codex/models?surface=app#app-gpt-61-terra] for guidance and
browser use [/codex/browser] and computer use [/codex/computer-use] for tool
setup and access requirements.

 2026-10-23 

 Codex CLI 0.164.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.164.0

 View details 

New Features

Save reusable prompts and slash commands in ten numbered /presets slots, with a keyboard menu for quick access. (#54380 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/54380], #54381 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/54381])

Set model, reasoning effort, speed, and permission profiles directly with inline /model and /permissions arguments. (#54378 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/54378], #54383 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/54383])

Preview viewed and generated images in the fullscreen transcript on terminals supporting Kitty or Sixel graphics. (#54216 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/54216])

Authenticate enterprise MCP connections using eligible Codex account grants or dedicated sign-in, including OAuth clients requiring secrets. (#55334 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/55334], #55859 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/55859], #56001 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56001])

Mask credentials during supported remote Bash commands and Zsh snapshot sessions, including credentials from custom providers. (#54309 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/54309], #54432 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/54432], #56049 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56049])

The Amazon Bedrock setup wizard is now enabled by default, with API key entry grouped alongside other AWS sign-in methods when credentials are detected. (#56033 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56033], #56023 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56023])

Bug Fixes

Failed compaction no longer leaves partial summaries in conversation history, and background compaction failures fall back without panicking. (#53932 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/53932], #55581 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/55581])

Resuming sessions now finds compressed histories and restores saved thread settings instead of replacing them with global defaults. (#54008 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/54008], #55923 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/55923])

Windows HTTPS and WebSocket connections now handle proxy authentication challenges using the signed-in user’s Negotiate or NTLM credentials. (#55994 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/55994], #56000 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56000])

CLI configuration overrides are preserved when -c or --config appears at multiple subcommand levels. (#56022 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/56022])

Queued prompts now wait for pending permission selections to resolve before continuing. (#55682 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/55682])

Hardened sandbox checks for case-variant metadata paths on macOS and replaceable symlinks used to locate executables. (#54273 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/54273], #55384 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/55384])

Chores

Reduced disk usage by pruning unused installations, cleaning abandoned marketplace checkouts, and bounding daemon logs. (#53827 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/53827], #55149 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/55149], #55689 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/55689], #55690 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/55690], #55856 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/55856])

Updated the embedded JavaScript runtime with safety checks for optimized Array.sort operations. (#54519 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/54519])

 2026-10-22 

 ChatGPT for iOS 1.2026.286 

New features

View and stop background commands from a new pill above the composer on supported remote hosts.

Mention chats from other connected hosts with @.

Improvements and bug fixes

A loading indicator now shows when saved messages are refreshing.

Activity groups are cleaner, and you can open images inspected by Codex on demand.

Unanswered questions remain available after a turn finishes and when reopening a chat.

More reliable replies to questions while another prompt is starting or finishing.

Autocomplete suggestions appear as each source loads, without waiting for slower sources.

Usage-limit explanations and reset times now appear directly in the chat.

Fixed prompts incorrectly appearing to fail after a response timeout.

Corrected extra diff lines after repeated edits to the same file.

Images in responses open in the full-screen gallery, and attachment removal targets the correct image.

The voice button returns after a call ends, so you can start another without reopening the chat.

 2026-10-15 

 Codex CLI 0.163.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.163.0

 View details 

New Features

Open /config to search preferences across categories, change supported settings immediately, and see which changes require a restart. (#52990 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/52990], #53429 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/53429])

Send a follow-up while Codex is responding to steer it immediately. Interrupting a turn also stops active Code Mode cells and their nested tool calls. (#51812 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51812], #51835 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51835])

Configure a leader key in /keymap. The default is Ctrl+X, shortcut hints stay open until you choose or cancel, and voice mute moves to F9. (#52273 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/52273])

Restart a managed background server from /daemon. Codex warns about work the restart could interrupt, then reconnects automatically. (#53173 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/53173])

Codex can run independent read-only tools in parallel, including skill, memory, and conversation-history lookups. (#52245 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/52245])

Daybreak is enabled by default for eligible accounts. codex exec can also select it with an OpenAI API key when the server offers access. (#53701 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/53701], #53717 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/53717])

Bug Fixes

Resuming a saved thread restores its permissions and network approvals, while respecting explicit overrides and current managed policy. (#53150 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/53150], #53419 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/53419], #53424 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/53424])

The network proxy now checks permission before a DNS lookup, rejects duplicate Host headers, and prevents credential aliases from bypassing inspection hooks. (#51650 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51650], #52661 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/52661], #53506 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/53506])

Return to an existing side conversation with /side after switching threads. Answers to pending prompts also go back to the thread that asked. (#52184 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/52184], #53436 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/53436])

On Windows, Ctrl+V and Shift+Insert paste text as well as images. On WSL, repeated pastes reuse the clipboard reader, and copy attempts time out instead of hanging. (#53224 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/53224], #52967 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/52967], #53453 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/53453])

Local commands finish promptly when a child keeps an output pipe open. Remote commands preserve late-arriving output so the final poll can still collect it. (#52652 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/52652], #53331 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/53331], #53622 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/53622])

codex exec no longer prints the same file diff again after an unrelated command. (#53444 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/53444])

Documentation

Updated the bundled OpenAI Docs guidance for migrations to the GPT-6 family, including model-specific reasoning, tool use, and data residency considerations. (#51768 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51768])

Clarified how the Linux sandbox uses bubblewrap for filesystem isolation and seccomp for syscall filtering. (#52610 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/52610])

Chores

Codex now uses its standard shell executor and no longer bundles a patched zsh. Configurations using the retired shell flags receive a migration notice. (#52160 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/52160], #52213 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/52213])

Reduced Linux and macOS release package size by stripping symbols from packaged binaries while preserving them in the original build outputs. (#52178 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/52178])

 2026-10-14 

 GPT-5.5 retirement in Codex 

Effective October 14, 2026, at noon PDT (19:00 UTC), GPT-5.5 retires from
Codex for users signed in with ChatGPT. The OpenAI API and Codex sessions
authenticated with an API key aren’t affected.

Use GPT-6.1 Sol (gpt-6.1-sol) when available to your plan and client.
Update workspace defaults, saved model settings, managed configurations,
custom agents, scheduled tasks, and scripts that still select gpt-5.5.

See GPT-5.5 retirement [/codex/models#gpt-55-retirement] for plan-specific
replacements and workspace model availability [/codex/enterprise/workspace-model-availability#prepare-for-the-gpt-55-retirement]
for administrator guidance.

 2026-10-13 

 ChatGPT for iOS 1.2026.279 

New features

File citations now open directly in the file viewer.

Collapse the entire Projects section to reach recent tasks faster.

Improvements and bug fixes

Faster task and thread loading, with saved task names and recent messages visible while reconnecting.

Markdown now renders in sent prompts, with questions quoted above your replies.

Queued messages appear immediately, with more reliable handling during turn interruptions.

Markdown files open in rendered view by default.

Clearer skill names and improved autocomplete after punctuation.

Fixed missing Rename prompts and usage percentages on iPad.

Plan mode stays selected when returning to the task list.

Custom permission mode now respects project-defined permissions.

Improved task widget reliability, including when local authentication is enabled for Codex.

Usage widgets adapt to accounts with only a weekly limit.

Connection errors offer a Retry action without hiding saved messages.

Fixed raw image URLs appearing in image-only messages.

 2026-10-09 

 Composer predictions in Codex 

Composer predictions adds autocomplete-style suggestions for your next message in
the Codex desktop app. After Codex responds, it uses the current conversation to
suggest a follow-up prompt in the message box. Press Tab to accept the suggestion
as an editable draft, or type your own message. Accepting a prediction doesn’t send
it for you.

The feature is available in beta to users aged 18 and older with personal ChatGPT
Pro plans in supported regions, in local and SSH threads with GPT-6 Astra and
GPT-6.1 Sol.

Learn more about composer predictions [/codex/features/composer-predictions].

 2026-10-09 

 Codex CLI 0.162.1 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.162.1

 View details 

Bug Fixes

Fixed a TUI crash when asynchronous questions contain multiple lines, preserving line breaks and complete hyperlink destinations. (#51866 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51866])

Fixed startup failures caused by differences between a running background server's feature settings and CLI defaults. Compatibility checks now apply only to explicit command-line feature overrides. (#52648 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/52648])

Full Changelog: rust-v0.162.0...rust-v0.162.1 [https://github.com/openai/codex/compare/rust-v0.162.0...rust-v0.162.1]

 Full release on Github [https://github.com/openai/codex/releases/tag/rust-v0.162.1] 

 2026-10-08 

 Codex app 26.1007 

Performance improvements and bug fixes

Fixed queued messages getting stuck on Sending and restored Steer in
secondary app windows.

Improved scrolling and navigation between files in the Changes panel.

Added clearer recovery guidance when a tool call needs approval but the chat’s
permissions prevent approval requests, with a link to those permissions when available.

 2026-10-08 

 GPT-6.1 Sol Ultrafast in Codex and ChatGPT Work 

Ultrafast mode speeds up token generation with GPT-6.1 Sol in Codex and
ChatGPT Work.

Ultrafast is available on Pro $500 and eligible Enterprise and Edu plans.
Enterprise access is off by default; workspace owners must enable it.
GPT-6.1 Sol Ultrafast supports inference residency in the United States and
Europe (EEA + Switzerland).

See Ultrafast mode [/codex/agent-configuration/speed#ultrafast-mode] for plan
and workspace requirements, Models [/codex/models#gpt-61-sol] for model
guidance, and Pricing [/codex/pricing] for usage and credit rates.

 2026-10-08 

 Codex CLI 0.162.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.162.0

 View details 

New Features

Add tools for creating and listing managed Git worktrees from trusted local projects when the worktrees feature is enabled. (#50148 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/50148])

Pin tasks in the agent Command Center with p and keep them in a shared Pinned group when supported by the server. (#51500 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51500])

Navigate and copy transcript blocks with /copy, use Ctrl+Insert to copy selections, and tune mouse-wheel scrolling with tui.mouse_scroll_speed. (#50434 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/50434], #50215 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/50215], #50209 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/50209])

Make URLs clickable in approval headers, questions, MCP prompts, warnings, banners, and verification prompts, including when links wrap across lines. (#51439 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51439], #51449 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51449], #51450 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51450], #51451 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51451], #51452 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51452], #51458 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51458])

Configure live web access and remote compaction capabilities for custom Responses-compatible model providers. (#50459 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/50459])

Add JavaScript helpers for streaming promise results as they settle, plus opt-in ranked tool search in Code Mode. (#51126 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51126], #51209 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51209])

Bug Fixes

Respect server model and reasoning-summary defaults for new TUI threads while retaining explicit launch overrides. (#50013 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/50013], #50811 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/50811], #50913 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/50913])

Preserve existing CRLF line endings in apply_patch updates without requiring an opt-in. (#51203 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51203])

Fix Linux sandbox startup with multiple denied files, reject writable sandbox-construction executables, and keep ripgrep configuration from weakening deny-glob masks. (#50059 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/50059], #51211 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51211], #51407 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51407], #51527 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51527])

Restore ordinary drive-letter file access on Windows 10 and match Windows sandbox temp permissions to the child process environment. (#51511 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51511], #51512 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51512])

Honor server Retry-After advice for retryable Responses and WebSocket failures. (#50418 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/50418], #51440 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51440])

Fix archive checksum verification when installing through Windows PowerShell with PowerShell 7 module paths present. (#51257 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51257])

Chores

Publish a signed PowerShell installer with Windows releases. (#51158 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51158])

Keep older stable releases and prereleases from replacing newer stable download targets or installer aliases. (#51186 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51186], #51425 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51425])

 2026-10-07 

 Codex app 26.1002 

Performance improvements and bug fixes

Fixed Windows sandbox refresh failures after app updates.

Fixed worktree creation and chat forking for WSL repositories whose paths
conflicted with Windows directories.

Kept the recent chats section collapsed when switching between chats.

Fixed Remove from section so it removes a chat from its sidebar section
without pinning it.

 2026-10-07 

 ChatGPT for iOS 1.2026.272 

New features

Page previews now appear inline in responses.

Added support for opening Codex task links directly on iOS.

Improvements and bug fixes

Redesigned the approval picker with clearer permission choices.

Find plugins from a single Plugins entry in the composer’s plus menu.

Faster streaming in long threads, with fewer freezes.

New tasks keep your selected computer while it reconnects or is offline.

Improved worktree support so files and changes match the checkout used on desktop.

Fixed task lists remaining empty after connecting.

Smoother keyboard transitions when starting tasks and swiping back.

Status updates no longer appear as questions that need an answer.

Clearer manual pairing code entry, including support for pasting formatted codes.

 2026-10-07 

 Codex CLI 0.161.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.161.0

 View details 

New Features

GPT-6.1 Sol is now the default model in the bundled and Amazon Bedrock catalogs. (#49318 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49318], #49339 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49339])

Amazon Bedrock supports multi-agent V2 and Ultra reasoning on compatible models; Bedrock Mantle also accepts AWS GovCloud regions. (#49345 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49345], #49813 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49813])

Sign in to MCP servers from an active terminal session with /mcp login <name>. (#49290 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49290])

Choose your microphone, speaker, and microphone input channels for voice conversations, with preferences saved locally. (#49437 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49437], #49836 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49836])

Daybreak is opt-in through --enable cli_daybreak or features.cli_daybreak=true; daybreak=true alone is insufficient. By default, controls and indicators are hidden, /daybreak is unavailable, and automatic Cyber routing is omitted—even for saved Daybreak threads. Saved preferences remain intact. Opt-in routing requires eligible ChatGPT sign-in, the OpenAI provider, and advertised model/program support. (#49856 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49856], #49858 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49858], #49859 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49859], #49861 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49861], #51207 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51207])

Select a Cyber access program per turn with codex exec --cyber-access-program or the TypeScript SDK’s cyberAccessProgram option. The explicit exec override remains available with cli_daybreak disabled and leaves the saved choice unchanged. (#49939 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49939], #51207 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51207])

Bug Fixes

Approved filesystem escalation can now grant broader write access while preserving denied reads and network restrictions. Background tasks retain their originating turn’s permissions. (#49353 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49353], #49880 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49880])

Explicit launch permissions survive terminal reconnects and new sessions, while implicit client settings no longer overwrite server or saved-thread web-search settings. (#49809 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49809], #49799 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49799])

Elevated Windows terminal sessions can start using an embedded server, and sandboxed PowerShell preserves relative paths beneath protected user profiles. (#49855 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49855], #49690 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49690])

Enter correctly submits buffered input after paste detection expires, including in Vim insert mode. (#49810 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49810])

Thread resume includes the latest committed history. Startup detects recoverable SQLite corruption earlier and preserves damaged databases as backups. (#49599 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49599], #49701 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49701])

Responses retries and WebSocket-to-HTTP fallback honor server retry guidance, reducing premature failures during overload. (#49441 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49441])

Documentation

Authentication guidance now accounts for keyring storage instead of implying credentials always reside in auth.json. (#49361 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49361])

Chores

Publishing an older alpha or hotfix no longer moves npm alpha tags backward. (#49704 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49704])

 2026-10-05 

 Codex CLI 0.160.1 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.160.1

 View details 

Bug Fixes

Preserve SYSTEMROOT, TEMP, and TMP when launching remote stdio MCP servers with explicitly configured remote environment variables, allowing Unix hosts to retain the Windows executor's startup environment.

Changelog

#51121 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/51121]: Backport Windows remote MCP environment preservation to 0.160.

 Full release on Github [https://github.com/openai/codex/releases/tag/rust-v0.160.1] 

 2026-10-02 

 ChatGPT for iOS 1.2026.267 

New features

New Home Screen widgets show recent tasks from your selected computers.

Track remaining usage and reset times with Home Screen and Lock Screen widgets.

Added a setting to keep the keyboard open after sending a message.

Improvements and bug fixes

Faster task list refresh when returning to the app, with existing task titles preserved.

Fixed missing tasks when loading or scrolling through task lists.

Smoother home screen, send, and keyboard transitions.

Canceling a queued prompt returns recoverable text and attachments to the composer.

Improved transcript stability when selecting text and loading older messages.

Worktree creation now respects your computer’s configured storage location.

 2026-10-01 

 Codex app 26.930 

New features

Added the option to move selected chats to a project at once.

Performance improvements and bug fixes

Improved recovery when attaching managed worktrees to chats.

Fixed large diffs appearing blank at some zoom levels.

Improved retries after queued messages fail to send or chats fail to open.

 2026-10-01 

 Codex CLI 0.160.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.160.0

 View details 

New Features

Browse older tasks in the agent command center with a keyboard-accessible “Show more” action. (#49106 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49106])

Select transcript text and paste with middle-click in fullscreen mode on supported local Linux X11 terminals. (#49112 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49112])

Start sessions outside a project with workspace defaults when policy permits, and restore saved permissions when resuming. (#49160 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49160])

Added opt-in Guardian review capabilities to retrieve earlier user instructions and include context from agent handoffs. (#49036 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49036], #49057 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49057])

Bug Fixes

Unsent queued messages now resume after reconnection once uncertain submissions are resolved, avoiding duplicate sends. (#49105 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49105])

The terminal UI now preserves server provider, reasoning-summary, and verbosity settings and shows the correct sessions in resume and fork history. (#49144 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49144], #49161 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49161], #49171 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49171])

Fixed Windows sandbox PowerShell fallbacks and long-path permission repairs, and suppressed unwanted console windows from background helpers. (#49019 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49019], #49058 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49058], #49098 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49098], #49164 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49164], #49386 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49386])

Subagents now retain environments that are still starting and receive their configuration or preparation failure. (#49075 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49075])

Prevented SQLite stalls during connection setup and logging, and surfaced initialization errors instead of masking them as timeouts. (#49032 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49032], #49102 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49102])

Explicit provider model catalogs no longer include unsupported bundled models or reuse stale entries after refresh failures. (#49135 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49135])

Documentation

Clarified how provider credentials use the configured storage backend and how env_key identifies the API-key environment variable. (#49118 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49118])

Chores

Reduced repeated plugin-loading work by caching parsed manifests and reusing HTTP connections for remote plugin requests. (#49099 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49099], #49100 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49100])

Added background reclamation of unused log database space to reduce disk usage. (#49069 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/49069])

Changelog

Full Changelog: rust-v0.159.0...rust-v0.160.0 [https://github.com/openai/codex/compare/rust-v0.159.0...rust-v0.160.0]

 2026-09-29 

 Codex app 26.928 

Performance improvements and bug fixes

Improved terminal startup in attached worktrees and fixed failures caused by
unavailable worktree environment settings.

Kept earlier answers visible when you send new instructions during a task.

Fixed automatic scrolling and prevented keyboard navigation through messages
from jumping back to the latest response.

Improved startup recovery when you return to the app window.

 2026-09-29 

 GPT-6.1 Sol in Codex and ChatGPT Work 

GPT-6.1 Sol offers near-Astra performance for complex work at a lower cost than
Astra. Consider it for repeated, long-running work across code, apps, and
documents. Availability depends on your plan, client, and workspace settings.

Use gpt-6.1-sol. See
Models [/codex/models#gpt-61-sol] for availability and
model selection [/api/docs/guides/model-selection#when-to-consider-gpt-61-sol]
to compare models.

 2026-09-29 

 Codex CLI 0.159.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.159.0

 View details 

New Features

Opt-in instant_interrupt lets new input steer Codex during model responses or long-running code-mode calls. (#48135 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48135], #48141 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48141])

New sessions get a compact welcome screen and consistent headers, with occasional tips during and after turns. (#48513 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48513], #48562 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48562], #48352 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48352])

The warnings viewer dismisses reviewed warnings when closed; press k to keep one for later. (#48205 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48205], #48206 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48206])

You can scroll the transcript while deciding whether to implement a plan. (#48805 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48805])

Native Mermaid rendering supports more flowchart edges, labels, and node groups. (#48814 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48814], #48895 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48895])

App-server clients can paginate thread history from a specific item. (#48151 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48151])

Bug Fixes

Windows launches avoid stray console windows for MCP servers, code-mode hosts, and piped commands; restrictive launchers can fall back to embedded mode. (#48138 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48138], #48238 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48238], #48483 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48483], #48491 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48491])

Copying transcript selections preserves Markdown tables, formatting, and significant whitespace. More terminals now copy automatically on selection. (#48548 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48548], #48549 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48549], #48469 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48469])

Blank sessions retain drafts when switching tasks, and threads can be archived and listed before their first turn. (#48628 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48628], #48828 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48828], #48199 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48199])

Local ChatGPT sign-in opens the browser reliably; onboarding also provides a shortcut to copy the login link. (#48502 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48502], #48544 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48544])

Approved commands retain explicit filesystem denials, and .aws directories are protected by default under writable roots. (#48155 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48155], #48176 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48176])

Fixed macOS TLS access in network-enabled sandboxes and remote environments that require proxy access. (#48565 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48565], #48198 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48198])

Chores

Removed automatic follow-up prompt suggestions and the tui.prompt_suggestions setting. (#48621 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48621])

Removed the bundled plugin-creator skill. (#48604 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48604])

Changelog

 Full release on Github [https://github.com/openai/codex/releases/tag/rust-v0.159.0] 

 2026-09-28 

 Codex CLI 0.158.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.158.0

 View details 

New Features

Configure copy-on-select and right-click paste in the fullscreen TUI. Copied transcript selections now preserve Markdown formatting. (#47639 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47639], #47896 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47896], #48118 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48118])

Connect to MCP servers that require pre-registered OAuth client secrets, including through codex mcp add --oauth-client-secret. (#47891 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47891])

Secure direct exec-server WebSocket connections with bearer tokens, including connections configured through app-server. (#47601 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47601], #47648 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47648])

Image generation and editing can explicitly request transparent backgrounds, and edits now accept file-backed conversation images. (#47484 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47484], #47956 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47956])

Terminal input approval is enabled by default for commands running with elevated permissions; runtime-only grants no longer cause unnecessary reviews. (#47799 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47799], #48073 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/48073])

Bug Fixes

Fixed Windows sandbox failures involving ordinary Windows 10 paths, rejected stored credentials, and large permission policies. (#47672 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47672], #47695 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47695], #47919 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47919])

Fixed Linux sandbox startup with nested writable roots and preserved Git metadata protections across writable roots on Linux and macOS. (#47623 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47623], #47974 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47974])

macOS patch operations now recognize system path aliases covered by existing permissions, avoiding unnecessary approval prompts. (#47879 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47879])

Approval reviews now retry when new user input arrives, so a status question does not automatically abort a pending action. (#47819 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47819])

Mermaid flowcharts now render quoted labels and ampersands; unsupported diagrams explain why they fall back to source. (#47572 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47572], #47678 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47678])

Command completion events now include early output and report process-launch failures to clients. (#47529 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47529], #47665 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47665])

 2026-09-25 

 Codex CLI 0.157.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.157.0

 View details 

New Features

Added GPT-6 Sol and Luna, including Amazon Bedrock support and migration prompts for older models. (#47332 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47332], #47347 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47347])

Enabled fullscreen transcripts by default and added Shift-click to extend text selections. (#47178 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47178], #47414 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47414])

Enabled automatic background-server startup for eligible interactive sessions, with recovery choices when server settings are incompatible. (#47179 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47179], #47318 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47318])

Added an f shortcut to fork conversations open in another app, preserving drafts and queued prompts. (#47185 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47185])

Made /import available in remote sessions and local background-server sessions. (#47317 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47317])

Improved terminal rendering with Unicode bullets, checkboxes, aligned equations, and optimization notation. (#47191 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47191], #47322 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47322])

Bug Fixes

Preserved active voice conversations when switching threads. (#47381 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47381])

Recovered unsent question answers into the composer when turns end, without disrupting active history searches. (#47422 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47422], #47423 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47423])

Respected tmux mouse settings and restored native scrollback for Terminal.app over SSH in automatic screen mode. (#47399 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47399], #47417 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47417])

Fixed configured proxy routing for realtime connections and standalone web search, including search redirects. (#47101 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47101], #47142 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47142], #47204 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47204])

Added retries for transient file-upload failures and increased the upload timeout to five minutes. (#47122 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47122], #47393 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47393])

Enforced network restrictions across redirects and ongoing HTTP and WebSocket traffic, including cancellation when policy changes revoke access. (#47389 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47389], #47407 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/47407])

 2026-09-23 

 ChatGPT for iOS 1.2026.258 

New features

Redesigned home screen.

New iPad split view keeps your task list beside the open task in landscape.

Added support for browsing changes inside nested Git repositories.

Improvements and bug fixes

Side chats now carry over attachments, selected text, and review comments.

Fixed replies appearing blank or incomplete when the next queued prompt starts.

New tasks preserve your selected computer and better recognize project checkouts and worktrees.

More reliable file links, with clearer loading feedback when opening files and new tasks.

Clearer error messages explain why task history could not load and what to try next.

Smoother animations when adding photos from the camera or Photo Library.

Fixed pairing failures caused by an incorrect device clock and SSH connection issues on Mac and Linux.

 2026-09-22 

 Codex app 26.917 

New features

Added a notification sound setting for task completions, permission requests,
and questions. Choose Default, Classic, or None. On macOS, you can
also choose a system sound or import an audio file.

Performance improvements and bug fixes

Added a sign-in recovery dialog for session errors that prevent app startup.

Preserved chat titles in the Rename dialog when worktree setup finishes.

 2026-09-22 

 GPT-6 Sol and Luna in Codex and ChatGPT Work 

GPT-6 Sol and GPT-6 Luna are rolling out to Codex and ChatGPT Work at lower
token prices than their GPT-5.6 predecessors. Use Sol for complex coding and
agentic workflows, and Luna for focused, high-volume tasks.

Both models are rolling out to Plus, Pro, Business, Enterprise, and Edu users.
Free and Go users can access Luna in the desktop app. Availability depends on
rollout and workspace settings; Enterprise administrators must enable the new
models. In ChatGPT, the models are available in Work and Codex, but not Chat.

Choose a model in the picker when available. In the CLI, use /model or start
a task with codex --model gpt-6-sol or codex --model gpt-6-luna.

See Models [/codex/models] for selection and migration guidance,
Pricing [/codex/pricing] for credit rates and usage, and
Workspace model availability [/codex/enterprise/workspace-model-availability#gpt-6-sol-and-luna-in-enterprise]
for administrator controls.

 2026-09-22 

 Codex CLI 0.156.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.156.0

 View details 

New Features

Choose an optional fullscreen UI with /tui for your next launch, including transcript search, mouse selection, and right-click copying. (#46732 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/46732], #46734 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/46734], #46883 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/46883], #46895 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/46895])

Voice conversations are enabled by default, with an F8 toggle, a /voice settings picker, and bundled audio runtimes for Linux and Windows. (#44921 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44921], #46071 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/46071], #44622 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44622], #44714 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44714], #44922 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44922])

Explore account usage, token totals, and plugin and skill activity through the /usage analytics dashboard. (#45764 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/45764], #45769 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/45769])

Filter tasks by status and create worktree sessions from the agent command center; worktree support is now enabled by default. (#46839 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/46839], #45276 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/45276], #44870 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44870])

Customize the terminal with six new themes and view supported Mermaid diagrams and display equations directly in responses. (#46504 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/46504], #46054 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/46054], #45612 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/45612])

Update the local background server through /daemon, or bypass it with --no-daemon. (#45854 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/45854], #46088 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/46088])

Bug Fixes

Preserve streamed answers and plans when turns fail, are interrupted, or receive subagent completion events. (#45549 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/45549], #46867 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/46867])

Fix clipboard forwarding in tmux and SSH sessions and preserve tab indentation when terminals send pasted text as individual keystrokes. (#45457 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/45457], #45454 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/45454])

Restore Plan mode when resuming sessions and preserve thread identity and settings when editing earlier prompts. (#45519 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/45519], #45845 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/45845])

Recover login through system proxies and refresh MCP credentials when OAuth discovery returns a 503 error. (#46562 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/46562], #44636 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44636])

Prevent speech from being dropped during playback pauses or bursts of incoming audio. (#46880 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/46880])

Close sandbox isolation gaps involving inbound Windows connections, privileged Linux/macOS sockets, and writes through read-only macOS file handles. (#44639 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44639], #45984 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/45984], #46500 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/46500])

Documentation

Clarify that deprecated friendly and pragmatic personality settings no longer select response styles. (#45809 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/45809])

Explain how ? matches a single character in network proxy allow and deny patterns. (#46027 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/46027])

Chores

Update bundled TLS dependencies, including OpenSSL 3.6.4 for Linux musl builds. (#45149 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/45149], #45489 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/45489])

Changelog

 2026-09-17 

 Codex CLI 0.155.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.155.0

 View details 

New Features

Added experimental /voice conversations with live transcripts and microphone controls on supported builds, enabled through /experimental. (#43581 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43581], #43651 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43651], #44331 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44331])

The TUI now shows live reasoning summaries in the status row and completion timestamps after successful turns. (#43558 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43558], #43921 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43921])

Added task hiding, archiving, and deletion in the agents overview, plus worktree ownership details and confirmed deletion of clean managed worktrees. (#43942 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43942], #44424 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44424], #44433 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44433])

Added Touch ID verification for MCP requests in local TUI sessions on supported Macs. (#43624 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43624], #43712 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43712], #43715 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43715])

Added configurable daemon update schedules and codex app-server daemon update; saved threads and active goals can recover after daemon restarts. (#43542 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43542], #43562 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43562], #44314 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44314])

Amazon Bedrock can now obtain AWS credentials from configured commands, with caching, expiration-based refresh, and authentication recovery. (#44028 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44028])

Bug Fixes

Accepted prompts are now saved even when compaction fails before a turn starts. (#44487 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44487])

Fixed missed tmux resizes, transcript viewport restoration, and stale history appearing after switching threads. (#43603 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43603], #43889 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43889], #43994 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43994])

MCP servers now report expired OAuth credentials accurately and provide reconnect guidance when token refresh fails. (#43947 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43947], #44359 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44359])

Automatic approval reviews now preserve complete actions and authorization evidence more reliably, retry transient failures, and distinguish review failures from unsafe-action findings. (#44482 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44482], #44569 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44569], #44570 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44570])

Switching accounts now invalidates remote-control sessions, cached WebSocket state, and model catalogs belonging to the previous identity. (#43906 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43906], #44341 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44341], #44489 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44489])

Blocked Windows-process escapes from restricted WSL sandboxes and hardened brokered shell snapshots against credential exposure. (#44286 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44286], #43909 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43909], #44040 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44040])

Chores

Aligned Python SDK and runtime publishing with stable CLI releases, using matching versions and verifying runtime assets before SDK publication. (#44067 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/44067])

Changelog

Full Changelog: rust-v0.154.0...rust-v0.155.0 [https://github.com/openai/codex/compare/rust-v0.154.0...rust-v0.155.0]

 2026-09-16 

 Codex app 26.911 

Performance improvements and bug fixes

Fixed back navigation getting stuck between settings and a chat.

Kept configuration warnings visible after app startup and window reloads.

Improved focus and alignment in answer fields for questions from Codex.

 2026-09-14 

 GPT-5.3-Codex-Spark deprecated 

GPT-5.3-Codex-Spark was deprecated on September 14, 2026. The research
preview is no longer available in the ChatGPT desktop app, Codex CLI, or IDE
extension.

Switch to one of the recommended models [/codex/models#recommended-models],
and update saved configurations, custom agents, and scripts that select
gpt-5.3-codex-spark.

For faster responses with a supported model, see
Fast mode [/codex/agent-configuration/speed#fast-mode].

 2026-09-14 

 GPT-5.5 retires from ChatGPT, ChatGPT Work, and Codex on October 14 

On October 14, 2026, GPT-5.5 will retire from ChatGPT, ChatGPT Work, and Codex
on all plans, including consumer, Business, Enterprise, and Edu plans. This
retirement does not apply to the OpenAI API.

For Codex with ChatGPT sign-in, choose an available replacement for your plan
and client. See GPT-5.5 retirement [/codex/models#gpt-55-retirement] for
migration guidance.

Update workspace defaults, saved model settings, managed configurations,
custom agents, scheduled tasks, and scripts that still select gpt-5.5.

See GPT-5.5 retirement [/codex/models#gpt-55-retirement] and
workspace model availability [/codex/enterprise/workspace-model-availability#prepare-for-the-gpt-55-retirement]
for migration guidance.

 2026-09-11 

 Quick chats with Pets and Appshots on Windows 26.908 

Chat while you work

Keep ChatGPT close while you work in other apps. In the ChatGPT desktop app on
macOS and Windows, you can now type a quick chat from the floating Pets controls
and press Enter to send it. Use @ to add context and $ to choose a skill.
Select the bell icon to follow progress in your threads, then select a thread
to open the full conversation.

Choose a companion or show just the controls in Settings > Pets. Press
Option+Space on macOS or Windows+Alt+P on Windows to show the controls
and focus Quick Chat. Pressing the shortcut again keeps Quick Chat open.
You can change Show pet in Settings > Keyboard shortcuts. To hide the
controls, select Hide pet in the command menu or Settings > Pets.

On macOS, you can also send an appshot to the floating controls. Keep the main
ChatGPT window in the background and choose Automatic for Appshot
destination.

Learn more about Pets [/codex/pets?surface=app].

Share an app window on Windows

Appshots [/codex/appshots] are now available in the ChatGPT desktop app on
Windows. Press both Alt keys to share the frontmost app window with ChatGPT,
then ask a question or describe what you want to do. An appshot includes a
screenshot and available text from that window. You can customize the shortcut
and choose which chat receives it in settings.

Other improvements and bug fixes

Open files directly from a conversation’s Sources panel, or download
files that ChatGPT can’t preview.

Assign a Codex Micro [/codex/features/codex-micro] key to Insert text and
add your own reusable text to the active prompt without sending it.

Reset a pet to its default size in settings. Custom pets also stay in place
when you refresh their artwork.

Keep unfinished comments on response text when you switch chats and return.

ChatGPT dictation now respects your saved Main language preference.

Keep tab widths and scroll positions more stable as you close browser tabs
in full and split views.

 2026-09-09 

 Codex CLI 0.154.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.154.0

 View details 

New Features

GPT-6-Astra is now available in the model picker and Amazon Bedrock catalogs. (#42879 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42879], #42619 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42619])

Experimental worktree support lets you create isolated checkouts for new or forked sessions using --worktree or /worktree, then browse and resume them. (#42652 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42652], #43069 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43069], #43120 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43120], #43286 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43286])

Answer questions inline while Codex continues working, using suggested choices or custom text without losing your main draft. (#42891 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42891], #42894 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42894], #42897 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42897])

Windows sessions can now share a background Codex server, with daemon lifecycle commands and managed updates. (#42405 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42405], #42392 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42392])

Vim editing gains R replace mode with undo and dot-repeat, plus more reliable Escape handling in legacy terminals. (#42194 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42194], #42584 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42584])

Copying responses preserves formatting in rich-text apps, and /copy can copy status output or individual session fields. (#42847 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42847], #43055 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43055])

Bug Fixes

Existing sessions pick up newly installed plugin tools and refresh skills and hooks after external plugin upgrades or rollbacks. (#42284 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42284], #42593 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42593], #42990 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42990])

MCP connections coordinate OAuth token refreshes and surface login challenges when refresh fails, without automatically replaying rejected tool calls. (#42413 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42413], #42552 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42552])

Startup avoids running workspace-controlled helpers before trust is established, and the macOS sandbox blocks terminal input injection. (#42324 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42324], #42590 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42590])

Remote resume and fork operations preserve saved permissions; fresh sessions and forks respect server model defaults unless explicitly overridden. (#43330 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43330], #43177 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43177], #43355 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43355])

Resuming a conversation open in another app now shows a read-only transcript with a retry option while preserving your draft. (#43253 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43253])

Automatic approval reviews better preserve authorization context through compaction and reject approvals invalidated by new user instructions or answers. (#42844 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42844], #42852 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42852], #43442 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/43442])

Documentation

Updated the bundled OpenAI Docs skill with GPT-6-Astra migration, compatibility, and prompting guidance. (#42931 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42931])

Chores

The deprecated codex mcp-server entry point is no longer available. (#42993 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42993])

 2026-09-05 

 Codex MCP server removed 

The codex mcp-server command and standalone codex-mcp-server binary have
been removed after their deprecation on August 24, 2026. Update integrations
that launch either command before upgrading Codex.

Use the Codex app server [/codex/app-server] for integrations. The app-server
command is experimental and isn’t supported for production workloads.

See the migration guidance [/codex/mcp-server] for details. Codex continues to
support connecting to external MCP servers [/codex/extend/mcp] through codex mcp.

 2026-09-03 

 Codex CLI 0.153.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.153.0

 View details 

New Features

Vim mode now supports undo with u and redo with Ctrl+R, preserving complete drafts including pasted content and attachments. (#41941 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41941], #42140 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42140])

The plugin CLI can list, install, and remove plugins from remote marketplaces. (#42150 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42150])

Set tui.auto_recap = false to disable automatic recaps while keeping manual /recap available. (#42101 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42101])

TUI history shows complete patches, input sent to background terminals, and individual completed commands. (#41893 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41893], #42107 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42107])

Plus and Team users receive an earlier warning when less than half of their allowance remains in an approximately five-hour usage window. (#42142 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42142])

Bug Fixes

TUI sessions reconnect after an external app-server connection drops, preserving drafts and transcripts while keeping uncertain or queued submissions paused for review. (#41911 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41911], #41916 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41916], #41918 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41918])

Full Access skips Guardian reviews for confirmation-only actions. User approval mode skips background Guardian scoring and prewarming, while sensitive-action checks and requests for user input retain their existing handling. (#42147 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42147], #42256 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42256])

Guardian review history survives compaction, restarts, and user-created forks while respecting rollback boundaries and isolating subagent history. (#41879 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41879], #42065 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42065])

Remembered MCP tool approvals are scoped to the selected app account, and relative MCP executable paths start more reliably on macOS. (#42133 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42133], #42117 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42117])

Rollout compression includes shared histories, codex exec resume handles compressed rollouts when selecting by working directory, and thread forks work with symlinked session roots. (#42039 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42039], #42135 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42135])

Configuration and API Updates

App-server thread metadata includes nullable model and reasoningEffort fields. Structured asynchronous questions are supported through request_user_input_async when enabled by the model catalog. (#42151 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42151], #42178 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42178])

tui.disable_paste_burst replaces the top-level setting, which remains supported as a fallback. (#41976 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41976])

Adds the disabled-by-default features.context_management.experimental_mode configuration. When enabled for eligible ChatGPT Plus, Pro, or Pro Lite sessions using the Codex backend, it activates token-budget context, history notes, and the new_context tool. API-key sessions, custom providers, and temporary structured threads remain excluded. (#42385 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/42385])

 2026-09-01 

 ChatGPT for iOS 1.2026.237 

New features

Attachments now work across all connected hosts, including Windows and Linux, and support videos from the Photo Library.

Press and hold the attachment button to attach recent photos.

A new Priority view brings running tasks, unread updates, and tasks awaiting your response to the top of the task list.

Task list loading and organization are faster and more reliable, with simpler date sections and fewer stalls or disappearing projects.

Reconnects are more reliable, resolving stuck Send states, missing approvals, and stale task updates.

 2026-09-01 

 Codex CLI 0.152.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.152.0

 View details 

New Features

Vim mode supports / and ? searches within drafts, highlighted matches, and repeat navigation with n and N. (#41586 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41586])

Rate-limit banners offer actions for checking usage, managing credits, resetting limits, and managing plans. (#41742 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41742])

The terminal UI and codex exec show credential-refresh progress, including Amazon Bedrock reauthentication. (#41239 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41239])

MCP server names can contain :, @, /, and ., supporting package-style names throughout CLI commands and authentication. (#41700 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41700])

Individual MCP tools support an output_token_limit setting, with consistent truncation across session resumes. (#41421 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41421])

App-server clients can configure thread/shellCommand timeouts, including deadlines longer than one hour. (#41384 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41384])

Bug Fixes

Vim-enabled composers now start fresh drafts in Insert mode, including after submitting messages or dispatching slash commands. (#41921 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41921])

Automatic approval reviews can retain longer messages and a larger conversation transcript. (#41931 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41931])

Automatic approval reviews preserve user instructions, answers, and valid authorizations across history compaction. (#41660 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41660], #41846 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41846], #41852 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41852])

Resumed threads restore their saved working directory when none is supplied, and client metadata updates preserve filesystem permissions. (#41567 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41567], #41464 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41464])

MCP tools remain available through cache refreshes and remote plugin changes; authentication retries use refreshed helper-provided headers. (#41336 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41336], #41344 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41344], #41396 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41396], #41400 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41400])

Opening the model picker refreshes available models without losing the highlighted choice. (#41467 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41467])

Fixed Windows sandbox execution with Microsoft Store PowerShell, subprocess hangs on terminal queries, and cursor-related display corruption in older JediTerm terminals. (#41227 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41227], #41436 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41436], #41673 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41673])

Cloud task requests reject untrusted backend URLs and disable redirects to protect saved credentials. (#41403 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41403])

Chores

The planning tool is disabled by default; enable it with tools.update_plan.enabled = true. (#41744 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41744])

Plugin recommendations begin loading during startup, reducing delays before the first turn. (#41375 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41375])

Full Changelog: rust-v0.151.0...rust-v0.152.0 [https://github.com/openai/codex/compare/rust-v0.151.0...rust-v0.152.0]

 2026-08-29 

 Codex CLI 0.151.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.151.0

 View details 

New Features

Added a configurable grace period for discovering tools from optional MCP servers. (#41199 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41199])

Extensions can now inspect or replace MCP tool results before they reach the model. (#41202 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41202])

Plugin catalogs now combine per-repository configuration and report invalid project marketplaces without hiding valid plugins. (#41208 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41208])

Bug Fixes

Preserved restored permission profiles across TUI turns and prevented /cd from weakening sandbox restrictions. (#41192 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41192])

Kept tool availability and reasoning effort correct when switching models or falling back to another model. (#41195 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41195], #41206 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41206])

Improved remote sandbox enforcement using the executor’s actual home directory, operating system, and path conventions. (#41196 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41196], #41204 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41204], #41207 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41207], #41209 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41209])

Preserved structured MCP tool and resource errors in app-server responses. (#41196 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41196])

Counted nested subagent token usage toward root goal budgets. (#41183 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41183])

Prevented stale Guardian classifications from authorizing actions after permission state changes. (#41196 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41196])

Chores

Added telemetry for escalated stdin reviews and remote executor MCP discovery. (#41189 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41189], #41205 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41205])

Stabilized Guardian WebSocket and core fixture tests under slow or highly concurrent CI. (#41191 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41191], #41194 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/41194])

 ChatGPT for iOS 1.2026.230 

New features

Added task search across titles and conversation content on connected hosts.

Added a compact composer gauge for viewing and adjusting reasoning effort.

Added a full-screen editor for longer prompts.

Inline visualizations now follow iOS appearance and accent settings.

 2026-08-26 

 Codex CLI 0.150.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.150.0

 View details 

New Features

Reference other Codex tasks with @ mentions, and ask agents to read, create, or message tasks from the terminal. (#40308 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/40308], #40315 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/40315])

/copy now offers a picker for full responses, individual code blocks, and blockquotes. (#39997 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39997])

Unnamed terminal tasks receive descriptive titles automatically, and /rename suggests an editable title based on the conversation. (#40492 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/40492], #40495 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/40495])

Markdown links appear as clickable labels in supported terminals, with visible URLs retained elsewhere. (#40471 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/40471])

Bind shortcuts to cycle permission modes, and use . in Vim mode to repeat your last edit. (#39873 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39873], #40521 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/40521])

New Interrupt hooks can run commands or MCP handlers when an active top-level turn is interrupted. (#40511 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/40511])

Bug Fixes

Untrusted projects no longer supply project-level AGENTS.md instructions, and managed deny-read rules remain enforced after permission changes. (#39837 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39837], #40004 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/40004])

Improved credential redaction in app-server diagnostics, including provider, authentication refresh, and attestation fields. (#39993 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39993])

Fixed remote MCP bearer-token lookup and required-server startup while preserving compatibility with older executors. (#39926 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39926], #39952 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39952], #39979 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39979])

Fixed elevated Windows sandbox setup and launch aliases under Unicode user paths. (#39971 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39971], #40570 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/40570])

Prevented Unix shutdown hangs caused by detached processes retaining a terminal or full terminal output buffers. (#40460 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/40460])

Fixed conversation compaction and multi-agent compatibility for Amazon Bedrock models. (#39804 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39804], #39825 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39825])

 2026-08-25 

 Browser extensions, site tools, and cloud sign-in 26.820 

More browsers: Use the ChatGPT browser extension [/codex/chrome-extension]
in Microsoft Edge, Brave, Opera, and Vivaldi, as well as Chrome. Set up
your browser in Settings > Computer Use in the ChatGPT desktop app.
All five support tab mentions and browser control; Opera doesn’t support
side chat.

Site tools (WebMCP): In the desktop app’s built-in browser, ChatGPT Work
and Codex can use tools provided by a website [/codex/webmcp] to work with
the page. Use GPT-5.6 Sol or GPT-5.6 Terra and update to the latest desktop
app. Site tools aren’t available with GPT-5.6 Luna or in Enterprise or Edu
workspaces.

Cloud browser sign-in: On eligible plans, ChatGPT Work on the web, iOS,
and Android can ask you to sign in to a supported
website [/codex/browser?surface=web#web-sign-in-to-a-website] through the cloud
browser. Enter your details in the sign-in flow, not in the chat. Cloud
browser sessions stay separate from your local browser. Website sign-in
isn’t available for Enterprise or Edu workspaces.

Availability depends on rollout and workspace settings. Website-access and
action-confirmation requirements still apply.

Performance improvements and bug fixes

Fixed browser tab mentions being lost when starting a chat from Home.

Improved right-to-left chat titles and Command+Arrow navigation in the composer.

 2026-08-25 

 Trigger scheduled tasks from Gmail, Slack, and GitHub events 

ChatGPT scheduled tasks can now run when supported events occur in Gmail, Slack,
or GitHub. Filter Gmail messages by sender or subject, watch selected Slack
channels, or respond to pull request activity such as reviews, comments, commit
updates, and merges.

Event-triggered tasks are available in ChatGPT on the web and mobile for
eligible plans. Connect the relevant app and approve its requested access before
creating a task.
The ChatGPT Slack app must be a member of each watched channel, and the
connected GitHub app must have access to each watched repository.

An event-triggered task can’t also use a time-based schedule. When matching
events arrive close together, ChatGPT may combine them in one run.
Open Scheduled to review pending events or choose Run now.

Learn how to trigger scheduled tasks from app
events [/codex/automations?surface=web#web-trigger-tasks-from-app-events].

 2026-08-24 

 Codex MCP server command deprecated 

The codex mcp-server command is now deprecated. Use the Codex app
server [/codex/app-server] instead.

Update, September 5, 2026: The command and standalone codex-mcp-server
binary have been removed. See the migration guidance [/codex/mcp-server].

 2026-08-24 

 Codex CLI 0.149.1 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.149.1

 View details 

Changelog

Full Changelog: rust-v0.149.0...rust-v0.149.1 [https://github.com/openai/codex/compare/rust-v0.149.0...rust-v0.149.1]

 Full release on Github [https://github.com/openai/codex/releases/tag/rust-v0.149.1] 

 2026-08-20 

 Codex and ChatGPT updates 

New features

Apple Messages: Use the Apple Messages plugin [/codex/plugins?surface=app#app-use-apple-messages-from-codex] to read and search Messages chats on your Mac and prepare or send messages. It’s available on all plans in the ChatGPT desktop app for macOS. You can use the plugin in ChatGPT Work and Codex. By default, ChatGPT sends messages only after you approve the message and its recipients. See the plugin guide for persistent-approval risks, revocation steps, and the known issue with tasks that disable approval prompts.

Site co-editing: Where Site collaboration is available, owners can invite active members of the same workspace as editors [/codex/sites#collaborate-on-a-site]. Editors can read the Site’s live database data, update the Site, save versions, and publish changes after the owner publishes the Site for the first time. Owners retain control of the audience, settings, analytics, ownership, version restoration, and editor access.

Editable Site URLs: Where URL editing is available, owners can change an existing Site’s ChatGPT-hosted address [/codex/sites#change-a-site-url] without creating another deployment. The previous address redirects to the new URL. Custom domains are a separate, existing feature and aren’t changed by this setting.

Computer History in Europe: Computer History [/codex/customization/computer-history] is now available in the EEA, Switzerland, and the United Kingdom for ChatGPT Pro, Business, and Enterprise users in the ChatGPT desktop app on macOS. It’s off by default and requires Memories. Business and Enterprise administrators must enable access before workspace members can choose to turn it on.

Shared thread snapshots: On all Codex plans, share a read-only snapshot of a local Codex thread [/codex/use-chatgpt#share-a-read-only-snapshot-of-a-codex-thread] from the ChatGPT desktop app for macOS. The snapshot doesn’t update when the original thread changes. Personal-account links can be opened by anyone with the link; workspace-account links are limited to members of the originating workspace. Codex redacts known secret patterns, but review the shared content because sensitive content may remain. View or revoke links in ChatGPT data controls [https://chatgpt.com/#settings/DataControls], under Shared links.

Unified pinned threads: Keep the same pinned chats [/codex/projects?surface=app#app-organize-projects-and-chats] across the ChatGPT desktop app and iOS.

 2026-08-20 

 Codex CLI 0.149.0 

$ npm install -g @openai/codex@0.149.0

 View details 

New Features

Added an interactive codex agents dashboard for searching, starting, opening, renaming, and stopping tasks, with configurable shortcuts. (#39094 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39094], #39112 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39112], #39114 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39114], #39142 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39142])

Added /cd, /pwd, and /cwd commands for managing the working directory in TUI sessions. (#38894 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/38894])

Added codex queue for sending messages to existing local or remote sessions. (#39092 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39092])

Expanded Vim editing with character replacement and more change motions such as cw, c$, and cc. (#39661 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39661])

codex doctor now diagnoses endpoint protection, network/proxy failures, desktop app state, and update connectivity. (#38827 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/38827], #38918 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/38918], #39060 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39060], #39074 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39074])

SDK users can now pass exact CLI config overrides and select max or ultra reasoning effort. (#38817 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/38817], #39662 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39662])

Bug Fixes

Queued messages now wake idle sessions reliably, resolve duplicate session names more usefully, and preserve pasted or deferred command semantics. (#39034 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39034], #39385 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39385], #39604 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39604])

Resumed and forked threads now restore their active permission profile instead of silently falling back to current defaults. (#39153 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39153])

Fixed duplicate sub-agent activity and tightened TUI routing for sub-agent notifications and approvals. (#39049 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39049], #39088 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39088])

Realtime WebRTC sideband connections now reconnect after unexpected transport loss without dropping pending output. (#39257 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39257])

Inline TUI history now remains available in Windows Terminal scrollback. (#39619 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39619])

Inactive TUI thread replay buffers are now bounded to prevent excessive retained streamed output. (#39081 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39081])

Documentation

Clarified that external contributions should go through issues and design discussion rather than pull requests. (#39089 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39089])

Documented DNS exfiltration risks and trust limitations for secure devcontainers. (#39283 [https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/39283])
